- excludes: PROPOSED Counting nested members/row fields/sequence positions as additional members of the outer set; counting duplicate insertion twice; shrinking on an absent query; changing the original set; or making size depend on the mutable state reachable through a member.

## EV-054 — DRAFT set members equation (K-14)

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED reviewed K-14 ordered-extraction equation. INFERENCE — The current R16 revision awaits independent review; any inherited positive review covers only its original scope and bytes.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build computing members(s) for a valid admitted finite set s under the conditional K-3 order
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:661–672 (EV-054, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:668–679 (EV-054); earlier source/review/decision provenance and standing as recorded in this pinned R4 base, not reopened original files. Current updates: SOURCE CARD:40–47; ARCH:21–30,44–80,118–121; six authenticated owner records and OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json. R16 current source: OWN_WRITE; ONE_BRIEF:399–405,413–418; C_WRITE_REVIEW:64–82; R13:992,994,996,1004,1006 (under review). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- text: members(s) returns the immutable row containing every member of M(s) exactly once in increasing K-3 order, and no other field, without changing s. A sequence occurs as its stable identity, including inside another immutable value; extraction neither replaces it by its contents nor uses its contents to order it. Equal canonical sets therefore have the same ordered member row, independent of construction history and later sequence-state changes.
- case: PROPOSED Apply F in both Sigma0 and Sigma1. members(E)=Row(), members(Sj)=Row(wj), and members(Uj)=Row(wj,b) for all j=0–4. In particular members(U0)=Row(a,b) even when their current contents coincide. insert(Sj,wj)=Sj and remove(Sj,b)=Sj exactly. The row obtained from each Sj in Sigma0 still contains the same wj in Sigma1, and a new members(Sj) has the same row value/order; no snapshot of a's old contents is substituted. Extracting Uj after the opposite insertion order gives the same canonical set Uj and exactly Row(wj,b).
- excludes: PROPOSED Omitting or repeating a member; returning Row(b,wj); sorting by insertion history, current sequence contents; returning recursively captured contents in place of identities; changing an old extracted row; or mutating the set during extraction.
- gaps: Preserve this DRAFT extensional equation and shared canonical set invariant for all incoming, literal and reachable-starting-state sets. The every-admitted-value all-depth member/query domain is decided; wrong-kind non-set operands return none under EV-084. Malformed admission, full traversal, canonical bytes, exact interfaces and complete result/temporary/work/debit costs remain E-4/E-14/E-16/E-18/E-27/E-28. ## EV-055 — Resolved Q2: each sequence has fixed length

- revision: 1
- standing: working policy — Q2/Q3 decision by Codex A under independently verified owner delegation; governs this draft, without adopting the additional DRAFT equations or an implementation.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under the narrowly resolved policy
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:673–684 (EV-055, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:680–691 (EV-055); earlier source/review/decision provenance and standing as recorded in this pinned R4 base, not reopened original files. - text: A sequence identity has one fixed finite length. Replacement of a held value preserves that length. A different-length sequence requires a distinct identity; no identity-preserving resize is admitted.
- case: Supply s of length 2. A permitted replacement keeps length 2 and the same s. A proposed extension retaining s with length 3 is outside this policy; a separately made length-3 handle is distinct.
- excludes: Changing the number of positions while retaining s, or retaining the old Q2 yes/no choice as an unanswered wait.
- gaps: Current policy: strict copy-then-row reading, fixed length, every-admitted-value all-depth set domain, start-derived logical scope, no whole-sequence write, invalid memo-mark refusal, position-size write charging with no sequence marker [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] and zero-origin positions. ## EV-056 — Resolved Q3: all-depth admitted-value set domain

## EV-062 — Start-derived logical sequence identity and nonrewindable supply (K-1)

- revision: 3
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED bounded K-1 core marked READY for drafting by C_R2/C_R3; no adoption or implementation acceptance. INFERENCE — The current R16 revision awaits independent review; any inherited positive review covers only its original scope and bytes.
- kind: definition
- subject: an evaluator build on an admitted complete fresh start, or a same-run resume distinguished as below, under the unresolved common canonical-byte/hash and collision/refusal premises
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:757–768 (EV-062, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:25,42,46–50,79–81; C_R3:16,22,35,41,82–92; ARCH:25–30; independently authenticated ZERO_START /owner_line and /authorizes_only; OLD_RULES:704–714. Older Arch coordinates in C remain provenance only. R12: A5_R2:54-170,174-289,424-428; G0181:9-14,37-39; C_ID:1,9,137-149; card0470. R16 current source: OWN_WRITE; ONE_BRIEF:399–405,413–418; C_WRITE_REVIEW:64–82; R13:992,994,996,1004,1006 (under review). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- text: PROPOSED A created sequence has an identity (rho,c): rho comes from that logical run's complete start under the common canonical-start/hash profile, and c is its creation count. Identical complete logical starts intentionally use the same rho; separate wall-clock invocations are not promised different names. A fresh entry begins with creation supply zero and an empty memo store. Replay of that fresh form uses its recorded complete start and repeats its creation counts. Imported identities retain their supplied pairs, and an imported pair must not be silently rebound to conflicting contents. A new creation consumes the next same-run count; the allocation high-water supply never decreases or reuses a consumed count, including after going back. A same-run resume therefore retains that high-water supply and its current run scope rather than pretending to be a count-zero fresh entry. Required framing, complete graph order, domain separation and collision refusal are obligations of the common profile, not a completed recipe here. A name correspondence used for a continuation fixes identities made before the point and preserves the order of later identities; order preservation alone does not preserve the charge of a name operation. SOURCE — Start normalization uses numbered local variables, retains represented definition/choice identities and includes every declaration, including unused. Sequence identity order is fixed independently of the current run by scope length, scope bytes, creation count. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — Exact codecs/hash/profile/admission remain prerequisites. PROPOSED — The hash in the complete canonical-start profile is HashProfile sha3-256-fips202-2015-bytes-v1. Computing rho still requires a complete reviewed canonical encoding and admission protocol. Preserve numbered locals, every declaration under Keep, equal-start logical naming, imported supplied pairs and high-water nonreuse. Identify the profile under the version-mark contract; no historical SHA-256 fixture becomes a SHA3 result.
- case: PROPOSED Two fresh entries with the same complete canonical start and two corresponding completed makes produce (rho,0), then (rho,1), under sufficient fuel. These are the same logical names across the two entries. In one of those logical runs, keep a point before those two makes, complete both, and go back under the separately specified paid restoration protocol: the next creation uses (rho,2), not (rho,0). An import presentation that assigns one supplied pair two different contents is refused rather than merged. This fixture does not calculate rho or its bytes.
- excludes: PROPOSED Adding an arbitrary per-occasion nonce to distinguish identical logical starts; equating distinct imported pairs merely because their current contents agree; silently merging conflicting state under one pair; resetting the same-run high-water supply on restoration; treating an order-preserving renaming as proof of equal fuel or equal out-of-fuel points.
- gaps: E-1/E-16/E-23 retain exact canonical program/value/start bytes, graph presentation, named hash/domain/collision/import validation and executable replay interfaces. The start-derived source is answered, and the core is drafted rather than deferred. Complete resumed/warmed entry records, allocation/rollback interaction with outside facts and the full continuation/projection/fuel theorem remain E-26/E-35–37. No distinct-occasion freshness, collision-freedom proof, complete meter, native result or phase authority is claimed. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L (former Rule S retired) [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. Remaining natural and byte entries, positive-loop setup/turn A/R/T/counter charges, separate admission resources, complete meter integration, EV-018/021 witnesses, exact spellings and any host calibration outside semantic units remain open. R10 N10 supplies the conditional fresh completed-unary EV-024/026 witnesses; incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration and general integration remain open. The comparison-event/frame schedule is now explicitly bound by the new draft set-search comparison entry and its pinned SV3/S4/M6 sources; all other conditional premises and meter, admission, identity and integration gaps remain. R12 EV-132 supplies a conditional candidate start/name fixture only; H, faithful full normalization codec, import/profile/collision/replay remain open. SOURCE — Numbered locals, Keep and fixed order are selected. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — current-scope equality is not automatically a proved collision. PROPOSED — The inherited unselected-H phrase is historical; the concrete hash subproposal is EV-129, still awaiting independent review/adoption. All other stated gaps remain.

- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:769–780 (EV-063, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:23,26,43,46,54,79–85; C_R3:15–17,49,82–92; ARCH:25–30,71–74,118–121; OLD_GAPS:251–259. R16 current source: OWN_WRITE; ONE_BRIEF:399–405,413–418; C_WRITE_REVIEW:64–82; R13:992,994,996,1004,1006 (under review). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- text: PROPOSED For this fresh-entry profile, program literals may contain no sequence handle, including at immutable depth. The initial sequence graph is the transitive closure from the input through rows, case payloads, set members and sequence contents; aliases and cycles are followed by identity, with one consistent state for each supplied pair. For every reached sequence the initial graph supplies its fixed length and all current held values. Missing contents, conflicting contents and inconsistent fixed lengths remain refusal grounds. SOURCE The last-place field and its admission predicate are removed. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] PROPOSED During execution, the complete live configuration includes control and continuation state, live bindings and frames, handlers, loop state, activation and use-once information, memo entries, pending/result roots, and every sequence reachable through those roots and held values, with its contents. Runtime allocation supply is recorded separately from the execution-state projection saved for restoration: the same-run high-water supply is never restored backward. Going back neither reinstates spent fuel nor erases the full trail. Detailed restoration must respect the separately specified activation/use-once/outside-fact boundaries; this definition does not supply that protocol. PROPOSED — A saved or resumed configuration binds the exact original hash, canonical-byte and meter profile descriptor. Missing or incompatible descriptors are not silently defaulted or converted into fresh starts. Profile checks do not reset scope, consumed creation supply or fuel. State fields must be reconciled with the reviewed current rules before wire-format work.
- case: PROPOSED Supply admitted input Row(s), where s holds Row(t) and t holds Row(s). The initial graph contains both sequences exactly once by identity despite the cycle. Omit t's contents, or give an alias of s conflicting contents: refuse the start. Put the same handle in a program literal with input 0: refuse it under this profile. At a later point retain a newly made u only in a live frame or memo/result root: u remains in the live graph. Restoring an older execution point does not reset the allocation high-water supply or refill fuel. No marker value is required or checked. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- excludes: PROPOSED Recording only top-level handles; dropping sequence-contents links, cycles, control/handler/activation/use-once state, live-frame/loop/memo/result roots; running with a missing or conflicting imported state; admitting a handle-bearing program literal under this input-only fresh-entry policy; confusing the saved execution-state projection with the nonrewindable allocation supply or an old fuel account.
- gaps: E-4/E-16/E-25 retain executable graph admission/decoder/encoding and exact complete-configuration presentation. The input/live root core and saved-state/supply split are now drafted. Checkpoint capture cost, one-phase contents-restoration integration [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json], interruption/committed-prefix behavior, ownership/ABA/concurrency and activation/use-once/outside-fact interaction remain E-35–37; no complete restoration engine or meter is defined. The later owner record removes the empty-marker convention and invalid-marker admission predicate; other edge results and admission refusals remain. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L (former Rule S retired) [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. Remaining natural and byte entries, positive-loop setup/turn A/R/T/counter charges, separate admission resources, complete meter integration, EV-018/021 witnesses, exact spellings and any host calibration outside semantic units remain open. The comparison-event/frame schedule is now explicitly bound by the new draft set-search comparison entry and its pinned SV3/S4/M6 sources; all other conditional premises and meter, admission, identity and integration gaps remain. R12 imported-state layout supplies only represented framing. Repeated pairs still need fixed length, all contents agreement plus all other admission conditions; full graph/profile/origin validation remains. - revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED fresh-only, build-relative record core marked READY by C_R2/C_R3; full evidence/replay forms remain open. INFERENCE — The current R16 revision awaits independent review; any inherited positive review covers only its original scope and bytes.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build producing or interpreting a record of a fresh count-zero, empty-memo entry
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:781–792 (EV-064, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:25,27,29–30,44,46,83–85; C_R3:17,82–92; ARCH:25–30,120; OLD_RULES:344–450; OLD_GAPS:261–269. R12: A5_R2:54-170,174-289,424-428; G0181:9-14,37-39; C_ID:1,9,137-149; card0470. R16 current source: OWN_WRITE; ONE_BRIEF:399–405,413–418; C_WRITE_REVIEW:64–82; R13:992,994,996,1004,1006 (under review). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- text: PROPOSED A record in this form names the executing build, program and input; holds the complete admitted initial graph and start-derived scope by value; explicitly records the count-zero/empty-memo entry policy; records the supplied fuel bound and terminal outcome; and retains make/write events in their actual order. SOURCE It carries no initial or final sequence last-place field. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] PROPOSED A successful outcome holds its returned value, interpreted against the final sequence state reconstructed from the immutable initial graph and ordered events. Out of fuel is recorded as that outcome, never a fabricated return. Start and event data are immutable by-value evidence, not live aliases; events retain enough information to reconstruct represented final contents and new sequences without consulting a later mutable heap. Preserve the richer immutable observation context: terminal/held outcome, request/reply and answer-pick correlation, consumption value for value and activation for activation, and outside replies' endpoint/version/state/row provenance. Exact projections and representations remain owed. This form is relative to the named build; import into a second build is not replay. Warmed/resumed entries need an explicit different form. An evaluator result alone does not establish a checker's separate statement-form/theory/scope soundness claim. PROPOSED — Every record additionally identifies its complete hash/canonical-byte/meter profile descriptor and retains that context for every nested identity reference. Build identification remains required and distinct. Missing or incompatible profile context cannot establish same-profile replay. Canonical field bytes and complete resumed/warmed forms remain open.
- case: PROPOSED A fresh run starts with input Row(s), s holding 0, zero supply and empty memo. It makes t, performs a write, returns Row(t), and records its build, immutable start, bound, outcome and ordered make/write information. Later mutation cannot rewrite that evidence. A successful contents(s) returns its immediate values without changing sequence state or adding a write; no final-marker field or marker-read event is needed. A stopped run records OUT_OF_FUEL and its committed prefix. These are record fixtures under the eventual complete meter, not numeric executions. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- excludes: PROPOSED Omitting the build or complete initial graph; replacing supplied fuel with remaining fuel; mutable aliases for historical evidence; inventing a return for OUT_OF_FUEL; interpreting a returned handle against later contents; pretending a warmed/resumed entry is fresh; equating import with replay or ordinary affirmative output with checker soundness. SOURCE No initial/final marker fields are retained. Removing them alone proves neither replay nor complete rich observations. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- gaps: E-12/E-16/E-26 retain exact record bytes, outcome/result presentation, parser/authentication/replay and complete state-qualified Eval inference. The fresh field core is drafted. ## EV-067 — Strict contents operational supplement

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — strict contents core, with decided empty result below. INFERENCE — The current R16 revision awaits independent review; any inherited positive review covers only its original scope and bytes.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build taking contents of an admitted sequence s of fixed length n
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:817–828 (EV-067, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:572–582; READ_VARIANTS:51–54,112–120; ARCH:48–53,104–105,120; DROP_STRICT /authorizes_only and independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1. - text: contents(s) returns an immutable row of s's immediate held values at that operation. For n zero it returns the empty row. It changes no sequence state. It does not replace any held value or add a write entry. A contained sequence contributes its stable handle, not a recursive frozen copy. Later writes to s do not alter this row's fields; later changes to a contained sequence remain changes of the same contained handle.
- case: For s holding Row(4,7), contents returns Row(4,7). Selecting zero-origin row field 1 gives 7. Replacing s's field later does not change that row. If the row holds an inner handle t, a later mutation of t does not replace that field's identity.
- excludes: A direct position read on s; returning a mutable sequence instead of the row; changing a returned immediate field after a later outer write; recursively freezing a contained sequence's state; permitting contents under a successful state-free memo proof.
- gaps: Listed operation invoices and atomic/dynamic charge stages are now DRAFT at EV-086–113; complete surrounding cost and host integration remain open. Reusing a snapshot is correct at a position only while its relevant held value has not changed; tracking own writes requires the actual program's bookkeeping and no intervening other-holder writes. A row of stable handles is not complete historical state evidence. ## EV-071 — Edge 2: zero divisor

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — inherited owner-decided zero result plus a separately reviewed PROPOSED zero-divisor tariff; full integration remains open.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build executing divmod(x,0) for admitted natural x under N15
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:865–876 (EV-071, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE EDGE_FIRST /owner_line,/authorizes_only; independently checked EDGE_FIRST_LINES:1 (8a39f730…18f7965); EDGES:98,105; ARCH:64; OLD_RULES:152–162 for row carrier order. Primary raw-line verification: OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json; all aliases bind INPUT_BINDINGS.json. R15: SOURCE EV_A6:40–244; G_RECHECK:13–26; OWNER_RECORD/OWNER_RAW (set choice only); N15. Exact alias pins are in the current INPUT_BINDINGS.json.
- text: SOURCE Division by zero returns the immutable row Row(0,x), quotient first and remainder second; this ordinary row result introduces no sequence mutation or write entry. PROPOSED Let a=ell(x). After wrong-kind classification, the zero branch uses (A,R,T,W)=(a+1,a+4,0,1), paid atomically under N15 before constructing any component. The quotient zero needs 1, the remainder x at most a, and the row 3. W=1 is a separate proposed tariff, not forced by the bound: substituting b=1 in the positive branch already gives aggregate R=a+4. A common length-only alternative retaining W=a for d=0 is possible but is not selected here. The positive branch's per-field remainder bound and r<d condition do not extend to d=0. Insufficient-fuel stopping before payment remains.
- case: PROPOSED D09 divmod(7,0)=Row(0,7) with 4/7/0/1, total12; D10 divmod(0,0)=Row(0,0) with 2/5/0/1, total8. At total the operation completes with zero remaining; one less stops with unchanged operation fuel and no component built. The inherited paid Row(0,7) result is retained.
- excludes: SOURCE / PROPOSED In a sufficiently paid invocation, ordinary none, a wrapped option, a sequence holding 0 and x, reversed fields, an ordinary divide-by-zero failure or a zero-divisor-specific stop. Do not exclude the ordinary insufficient-fuel stop, treat zero division as Rule E, or describe W=1 as mathematically forced.
- gaps: The zero result stays decided and the W=1 tariff stays PROPOSED; the common length-only W=a alternative is recorded but not selected. EV-011 supplies the positive branch conditionally. E-2 retains adoption/integration limits and other edge charges; no r<0 condition follows. Inherited R6/R10 accounting below is historical and is narrowed only by the R15 entries above. ## EV-072 — Edge 3: right shift past the operand length

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — owner-selected five-operation set and retained overshift result; candidate equation and tariff transcribed with their proposal/integration limits.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build executing shift_right(x,k) on already obtained admitted naturals under N15
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:877–888 (EV-072, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE DROP_STRICT /owner_line,/authorizes_only; independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1 (de13da5f…037a8e5); EDGES:106; ARCH:65; OLD_GAPS:61–69 retains bit vocabulary work. Primary raw-line verification: OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json; all aliases bind INPUT_BINDINGS.json. R15: SOURCE EV_A6:40–244; G_RECHECK:13–26; OWNER_RECORD/OWNER_RAW (set choice only); N15. Exact alias pins are in the current INPUT_BINDINGS.json.
- text: SOURCE The selected set includes shift_right. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bitops-method-hold-more-lanes.json; all five bit operations built in as EV-A6 revision 1 proposes] PROPOSED The EV_A6 candidate meaning is x divided by 2^k, rounded down. With a=ell(x), charge (A,R,T,W)=(a+ell(k),max(1,a-k),0,max(1,a-k)) under N15. For k>=a the result is zero, including the equality case; the already decided k>a zero result is retained. No sequence mutation or write entry is introduced. Classify admitted wrong kinds first with actual-part Rule E.
- case: PROPOSED shift_right(40,3)=5;8/3/0/3;total14. shift_right(40,6)=0 at k=a;9/1/0/1;total11. shift_right(40,7)=0 at k>a;9/1/0/1;total11. Each has the exact-total and one-less controls of N15; the general paid overshift-zero witness remains.
- excludes: SOURCE / PROPOSED Returning none solely because k exceeds or equals the operand length, a signed/host-width shift, truncating k to a host word, or treating the old overshift default alone as authority for all names/equations. The separate owner record selects the five-operation set; final spellings and unselected bit-length vocabulary remain open.
- gaps: R15 transcribes the right-shift equation, zero/equality convention, candidate tariff and three operation-stage cases. E-6 is no longer a five-operation-set owner wait; final spelling/profile, codec, admission/meter integration, no-bit-length operation and assurance remain E-4/E-6/E-18/E-27/E-40. No evaluator conformance vector has run. Inherited R6/R10 accounting below is historical and is narrowed only by the R15 entries above. ## EV-073 — Edge 4: huge left shift uses resource precharge

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — owner-selected five-operation set and retained no-fallback/precharge rule; candidate equation and tariff retain proposal/integration limits.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build executing shift_left(x,k) on already obtained admitted naturals under N15, including an unpayable prospective result
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:889–900 (EV-073, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE DROP_STRICT /owner_line,/authorizes_only; independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1 (de13da5f…037a8e5); EDGES:107; ARCH:66; OLD_RULES EV-017–023 and OLD_GAPS:61–69. Primary raw-line verification: OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json; all aliases bind INPUT_BINDINGS.json. R15: SOURCE EV_A6:40–244; G_RECHECK:13–26; OWNER_RECORD/OWNER_RAW (set choice only); N15. Exact alias pins are in the current INPUT_BINDINGS.json.
- text: SOURCE The selected set includes shift_left. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bitops-method-hold-more-lanes.json; all five bit operations built in as EV-A6 revision 1 proposes] PROPOSED The EV_A6 candidate meaning is x*2^k. With a=ell(x), charge (A,R,T,W)=(a+ell(k),a+k,0,a) under N15, where k is the shift amount itself, not its bit length. Charge the full prospective a+k even when x=0. SOURCE / PROPOSED The inherited no-fallback rule remains: obtain and charge a safe bound before constructing the result; if the required bound cannot be obtained or paid, stop as out of fuel with nothing built by this primitive and retain the prior committed prefix. This particular a+k formula is exact natural arithmetic and does not provide EV-021's unavailable-bound execution witness.
- case: PROPOSED shift_left(5,3)=40;5/6/0/3;total14. shift_left(0,4)=0;4/5/0/1;total10, with no zero discount. shift_left(1,64)=2^64;8/65/0/1;total74. Symbolically shift_left(1,2^64)=2^(2^64);66/(2^64+1)/0/1;total2^64+68. The huge result is not built here. Each has N15's exact-total/one-less control as a conditional operation-stage statement; a runtime numeric conformance result is not claimed.
- excludes: SOURCE / PROPOSED Substituting zero or none for an unpayable result, using ell(k) in place of k in R, zero-result/canonical-reuse discounts, construction before payment, a maximum shift amount/host width, or treating huge alone as a fixed outcome at every budget. T=0 does not prove no scratch-space use.
- gaps: R15 transcribes the left-shift equation, exact a+k prospective bound and candidate tariff. The result and invoice can be described symbolically without allocating the huge result. E-6/E-18/E-27 retain integration/assurance; E-4/E-40 retain codec/spelling; positive T and EV-021's unavailable-bound witness remain NOT DONE. No maximum natural or adopted tariff follows. Inherited R6/R10 accounting below is historical and is narrowed only by the R15 entries above. ## EV-086 — K-13 argument parts

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: definition
- subject: a listed K-13 operation after its argument values are obtained
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:36,44–46; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports. R11: A2_R1:49-61,65-84,89-119,123-149,161-179; G0182:13-17,25-55; G_A2_R0:154-160; card0469.
- text: The part of an admitted natural is its bit length, with zero counting as 1. The part of a bytes value is its length plus 1. The part of every other admitted value is 1. Each listed operation uses these parts in its A entry, subject to Rule E for decided none-producing edges. R11 conditional bytes exception: only the proposed valid bytes get/slice entries EV-126/127 charge their receiver A=1 rather than its full part; concat/hash still use full parts. The bytes part remains length+1, and outside or wrong-kind Rule E still sums the actual complete parts of every supplied operand. This is a proposed semantic tariff, not a representation, scan-work or native bound theorem.
- case: parts(0)=1, parts(7)=3, parts(Bytes(ab cd))=3, and parts(Row(4,7))=1. These are argument components, not complete operation or program invoices.
- excludes: Counting natural zero as zero bits; charging a bytes argument only by its length; recursively counting a row merely to obtain its part.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. Remaining primitive entries, positive-loop invoices, separate admission resources, complete integration and physical calibration outside semantic units remain open. Nonempty mixed-kind comparison events and logical frames are specified by the new draft set-search comparison entry; complete numeric search oracles still require explicit admitted operands, canonical member order, search path, invoice and fuel premises. ## EV-088 — K-13 temporary-frame component

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: definition
- subject: a listed nonempty set-search operation
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:38,65–68,88; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: For set search, T is the following once-per-search reservation in logical frame units, not the observed peak P. For nonempty set search it is the smaller of the sought value's height and the tallest member's height, plus 1. This precomputed comparison-frame bound is reserved once for the search, rather than summed again for every comparison. Empty-set operations with no comparison have T=0.
- case: Given stated height premises h(v)=2 and maximum member height 3, the nonempty-search component is T=min(2,3)+1=3. An empty-set member query instead has T=0. This component example is not a complete nonempty-search fuel oracle.
- excludes: Using zero merely because values are interned; summing separate simultaneous-frame bounds over all comparisons; interpreting the bound as measured native storage.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. ## EV-091 — K-13 dynamic comparison prefix

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.

- text: After the atomic known invoice, comparison events are paid one at a time before each event. No result or state change is committed until every required comparison event completes. This checks the paid-prefix rule only; it is not a numeric nonempty-set oracle or an EV-021 unavailable-bound witness.

## EV-092 — K-13 Rule E actual-argument edge invoice

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion. INFERENCE — The current R16 revision awaits independent review; any inherited positive review covers only its original scope and bytes.
- kind: requirement
- subject: a primitive with a decided ordinary none result for wrong kinds or out-of-range read/slice/write
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:44–46,60,62,69,72,75,78,80,90,93,100; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports. R16 current source: OWN_WRITE; ONE_BRIEF:399–405,413–418; C_WRITE_REVIEW:64–82; R13:992,994,996,1004,1006 (under review). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json]
- text: After evaluating its arguments, classify operand kinds and range before result-size or search calculations. On such a decided edge, A is the sum of parts of all arguments actually supplied, R=0, T=0 and W=1. A+1 is paid atomically or not at all. Success returns the already-existing bare none and changes no cell or primitive trail entry; earlier argument effects remain.

## EV-114 — Natural addition equation and invoice

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of the positively qualified EV-A1 proposal with Q F1–F9 carried; independent EV-G2 review still required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: add(x,y) after evaluation of two admitted operands, under N8
- from: SOURCE R8_A1:34–47,65,78–82,95; R8_Q:15–16,20–24,32–38 (F1–F7); R8_METER:36–44; R8_G0176:1,59 for the base only; R8_PLAN:106–107.
- text: For admitted naturals x,y, add(x,y)=x+y. A=a+b, R=m+1, T=0, W=m under N8. Since x,y<2^m, x+y<2^(m+1), so R covers the result including zero. There is no valid-domain exceptional pair; admitted wrong kinds instead use Rule E, not a valid-add invoice.
- case: Conditional operation-stage fixtures under N8. A01: add(0,0) -> 0; A/R/T/W=2/2/0/1, total 5; A02: add(1,1) -> 2; A/R/T/W=2/2/0/1, total 5; A03: add(3,0) -> 3; A/R/T/W=3/3/0/2, total 8; A04: add(4,0) -> 4; A/R/T/W=4/4/0/3, total 11; A05: add(7,1) -> 8; A/R/T/W=4/4/0/3, total 11; A06: add(Row(),1) -> none; A/R/T/W=2/0/0/1, total 3. Every displayed total is sufficient only for that primitive with obtained operands; total minus one fails atomically.
- excludes: Returning none on valid naturals; a wrapped/negative result; value-dependent discounts for zero, shorter results or reuse; omitting any known tariff or debiting it twice; treating wrong-kind Rule E as free.
- gaps: E-3/E-18/E-27 retain the other natural/byte entries, unit, complete syntax/interfaces and surrounding structural/admission charges. No fifth operation or whole-run calibration is supplied.

## EV-117 — Natural-only comparison equation and invoice

## EV-119 — Conditional global collection, roles and lexical scopes

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED conditional transcription of reviewed A4 R2; exact choices and independent EV-G2 review remain.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under a fixed candidate source profile, without adoption
- from: SOURCE R9_A4:132–158,268–269; R9_F0264:7,11,15; R9_PLAN:101,103,106–107; R9_G0178:1,7–18; card 0466.
- text: PROPOSED — Before any reference resolves, collect active primitive arities, built-in choice counts and every program choice/definition count into one table. Global declarations are pairwise distinct and unreserved. Parameters cover the whole definition body; let binds only its continuation, not initializer; branch binds only its own body, and else nothing; both loop names bind only its body, not bound or initializer. No binder escapes. Each variable spelling is bound once per definition and is never global. Calls require primitive/definition role and exact arity; constructors and case heads require choice role. Missing/wrong-role globals and held words are refused before the run. Plain REF atoms use only visible binders, never the global table, and missing binders are refused. Forward global references are allowed without reordering declarations. Every case ordinal is below its choice count, none repeats, and else is required unless all cases are covered. Resolve once before running, to fixed references, never by runtime spelling lookup.
- case: PROPOSED — S01 def f(x) = x resolves its local binder although x is absent from the global table. S02 let x = 0; x resolves only the continuation; S05 let x = x; x is refused for the initializer use. S04/S06–S09 refuse earlier, sibling/else, bound/initializer or escaped uses. N01 resolves a forward choice; N02 resolves a forward definition; N03 resolves a forward choice inside a literal. These are name derivations, not executions.
- excludes: PROPOSED — Global-table lookup of local variables; definition-wide binder visibility; shadowing/capture under this candidate; resolving only already-seen globals; silently reordering totality order; wrong-role/arity calls becoming ordinary none; runtime display-name rediscovery.

## EV-121 — Conditional conservative classifier and whole-action gate

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED conditional transcription of reviewed A4 R2; exact choices and independent EV-G2 review remain.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under a fixed candidate source profile, without adoption
- from: SOURCE R9_A4:213–251,265–267,271,335; R9_F0264:8–9,15–17,23–25; R9_PLAN:101,103,106–107; R9_G0178:1,7–18; card 0466. R14 sources: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ1, DIGEST.json#F036.
- text: PROPOSED — For a resolved first-order definition f, its closure contains f and every definition called anywhere in any closure body, including all arguments, dead branches and recursive components. It passes exactly when no body names the fixed make, contents or write roles; unresolved callees have already been refused as text and cannot pass. Every make is forbidden even empty or unused. Immutable field/byte access has no sequence fallback; fixed length, same and handle carriage may pass without mutable-state observation. A paid immutable invocation outside its required operand domain returns none without primitive state changes, but valid reads can also return stored none. Sequence identities may occupy legal set member/query positions; a required set operand must still be a set. Prior argument effects stand, and an immutable outer call excuses none of its arguments. A failing memo mark refuses before the run. Every definition invoked by admission or result checking, including literal, constructor-field and membership checks and all callees, must pass marked or not. An unmarked stateful definition may be offered in an ordinary separately fuelled run under its other conditions; it has no admission exemption. This is exact for the syntactic forbidden-role predicate and conservative for semantic state independence; it is distinct from syntax-totality and provides no positive-loop equation. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition] Gate PASS is only the stated effect predicate, not evidence of these additional reuse premises and not a new memo-mark refusal policy. PROPOSED — It also does not establish the whole checked-action row or termination: each invoked check and its rejection/exhaustion possibilities remain E-21. [digest: DIGEST.json; F036]
- case: PROPOSED — N9 C1–C13 are the thirteen explicit gate derivations: pure field/length/carriage/comparison and pure recursive closures pass; direct/helper/dead-branch contents, allocation and write fail. C12 has effect FAIL with no invalid mark to reject in the ordinary context, but fails admission/result eligibility. X2/X4 show that a safe outer operation or deep helper cannot hide forbidden work; X3 is a pure recursive component that passes only this effect predicate. No case claims successful evaluator execution or a complete fuel budget.
- excludes: PROPOSED — Path-pruning dead effects; omitting recursive/helper closure; pardoning argument effects; equating none with invalid operands in both directions; accepting a sequence as a required set receiver; applying the gate only to marked admission helpers; replacing syntactic conservatism by semantic completeness; treating gate PASS as totality, fuel success or adoption.
- gaps: E-4 complete presentation/admission and grammar proof; E-6 vocabulary; E-11 syntax-total implementation; E-13 memo policy/resources; E-16 encoding; E-18 interfaces; E-22/O1 general positive equation beyond selected bound two and positive-loop charges; E-24 executable visiting/resolution; E-33 gate integration; E-40 all exact word choices. All inherited meter, witness and reconsideration obligations remain. SOURCE — The former O1 owner wait is answered only at bound two (0 then1). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-loop-o1-a-and-arch-fixes.json; E-22/O1 A, bound two only] PROPOSED — General positive equations and positive-loop setup/turn/counter charges remain open; spelling/profile and other existing technical holds are unchanged.


## EV-122 — Structural events and ordinary/memo call composition

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of the positively reviewed EV-A3 R1 proposal; independent EV-G2 review required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: covered evaluator structural and call events after their required predecessors
- from: SOURCE A3_R1:47-62,66-76,84,91-94; Q0097:12-13,17,21-36; G_A3:23-29,104-108,138; DESIGN_R5:923-933; R9_RULES EV-016/025/043/113 R14 sources: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ1, owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ3, DIGEST.json#F014.
- text: PROPOSED Under this DRAFT schedule, variable reads and already-admitted program literals each have (A,R,T,W)=(0,0,0,1). A strict let has the same own invoice after its initializer and before binding or body; the initializer runs even if unused. An ordinary non-memo call pays (0,0,0,1) after all arguments and before the body. Parameter binding, return and the semantic call frame are covered by that proposed tariff. These zero structural cells are proposed here: the inherited call unit did not force them or prohibit another T proposal. A memo lookup pays (0,0,0,1) after arguments. For an eligible completed hit whose result reuse satisfies OQ1, that one lookup unit is the whole call-related charge under proposed E-20 candidate A. On a miss, pay the additional (0,1,0,1) atomically after lookup and before the body: call W1 and prospective memo-entry cell R1. Reserving that cell is distinct from installing or reusing a completed result. Case keeps its inherited dispatch invoice, including branch binding; subject and selected body remain separately evaluated. Pay each step after its evaluated predecessors and before successors, retaining an already committed prefix on a later stop. Full prospective constructor R is charged even on canonical reuse. Known invoices use safe uncapped exact arithmetic and atomic payment; known W is not debited twice. All these additions are proposed composition, not adoption of E-20 A or the cell fee. SOURCE — A memo call reaching its own unfinished key has no completed hit and runs under ordinary fuel; it does not reuse an unfinished result or stop early as unknown solely because that key is unfinished. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A] The additional reservation above remains a conditional tariff proposal; OQ3 does not adopt lookup, miss, entry-cell or restoration fees. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition]
- case: PROPOSED N10 N1 has total4 and an arithmetic-only omission comparison3. N2 has total4 versus3 when only the let fee is free; at fuel3 the proposed schedule stops at the final literal. W22/W24/W26i/W26ii below are conditional whole-run witnesses under this schedule and the inherited operation premises. SOURCE — When an eligible memo call g(4) reaches its own unfinished key, the inner call continues as an ordinary fueled execution; an implementation returning a hit or immediate key-triggered unknown fails OQ3. INFERENCE — In the explicit fixture g(x)=g(x), repeated re-entry eventually exhausts finite ordinary fuel under the conditional positive-call meter. This case supplies no exact numeric budget or universal divergence claim. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A]
- excludes: PROPOSED No extra ordinary-call debit on an A-profile hit; no skipping strict initializers, constructor-reuse discount or partial miss payment. No completed result from an incomplete entry. SOURCE — Immediate unknown solely at an in-progress key is the dropped OQ3 B branch. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A] PROPOSED — This schedule covers only the permitted M-C one-argument memo calls. Zero-argument and several-argument memo calls are outside the current profile; explicit author rows are ordinary unary arguments. Interrupted/warmed/resumed/restoration contracts and exact fees remain open at E-13/E-20. [SOURCE: owner-record-2026-11-12-memo-key-delegated-mc.json, authorizes_only; DECISIONS.postimage.md:39,51; BRIEF-MEMO-KEY.md:18,28,32-35.] No proved bound for covered administrative work follows.
- gaps: E-3/E-13/E-20/E-27 retain adoption/integration and alternative-composition debt; E-34/E-37 restoration; static bound assurance and complete meter remain open.

## EV-124 — Entry, return and partial start-admission boundary

- revision: 3

- text: PROPOSED Propose run entry as the first execution event, (A,R,T,W)=(0,0,0,1), covering input binding irrespective of still-unselected multiple-input packing. Returning the run result adds (0,0,0,0). Start admission is proposed outside execution fuel. Its partial list includes input/program-literal interning, case name/count/number checks to every depth, refusal of incoming sequences carrying the computed current scope or reachable sequences lacking contents and scope-hash calculation. This list is not exhaustive: preserve all other program/value/graph validity, declaration, alias/contents consistency, cycle handling, sequence-free program-literal and memo-mark refusal obligations. Refusal before execution, ordinary results and execution exhaustion remain distinct. The complete admission representation, traversal, canonical start/hash and separate admission-resource/exhaustion protocol are missing; no linear bound or start-size cap is supplied. A D-ADM-5 evaluated-definition check is a separate run with its own fuel and record under the same proposed execution schedule; exhausting it is unknown, not no. Such checks retain the mandatory transitive state-free gate and do not finish preliminary admission's protocol. SOURCE — Incoming mathematical naturals have no selected size cap; they are limited by required paid resources. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json; OC-03 A] PROPOSED — Finite representation/admission/resource/exhaustion contracts remain open; this is no free admission or universal successful-admission guarantee. PROPOSED — Preliminary scope hashing uses the same HashProfile over separately specified canonical-start bytes. Its admission-resource budget and inability/refusal protocol must be supplied separately; EV-129's callable execution tariff does not make admission free or price it by implication.
- case: PROPOSED N1-N7 each include entry1 exactly once. A run can exhaust at entry with zero execution fuel after a stipulated successful admission; that is not a universal outcome for invalid or validation-resource-limited starts. Returning an already obtained result adds no new return debit.
- excludes: PROPOSED No complete admission algorithm, universal preliminary-refusal resource guarantee, invented mathematical start-size restriction or capped incoming-natural domain, free evaluated-definition checker, serialized outcome schema or multi-input packing. This is a boundary proposal, not a native resource proof.
- gaps: E-3/E-4/E-12/E-13/E-16/E-24/E-26/E-33 retain complete admission, invocation and evidence debt. ## EV-128 — Binary bytes concatenation and invoice

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — conditional transcription of positively reviewed EV-A2 R1; independent EV-G2 required; no adoption.

- subject: two obtained bytes values a and b
- from: SOURCE A2_R1:49-52,80-85,117,141-143,179; G0182:23,27,35,55; PLAN:99,106-107
- text: PROPOSED For bytes a and b, concat(a,b) is the bytes of a followed by those of b, with length len(a)+len(b). Propose (A,R,T,W)=((len(a)+1)+(len(b)+1),len(a)+len(b)+1,0,0): full parts for both arguments and full prospective bytes-result cells, even on reuse. The R convention covers writing these cells; W zero here is not a physical-copy theorem or omission of all construction cost. This is a binary operation; longer joins use nesting with each invocation's separately evaluated operands and invoice. Wrong kinds use actual-parts Rule E and paid bare none. Evaluate arguments left to right, classify kinds first, compute the uncapped exact invoice safely, pay atomically, then construct; an unpayable primitive builds nothing and does not erase completed argument effects.
- case: PROPOSED N11 T15–T17 cover empty, ordinary and wrong-kind concatenation. Empty with empty returns empty bytes at own invoice3, including prospective R1. Slice/rejoin and one-byte slice/get agreement use the selected start/length slice and natural byte-read meanings; invoices and boundary integration remain proposed. SOURCE — [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-08 A; OC-09 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json]

- gaps: The bounded binary entry is proposed, not adopted; E-3/E-27 full-meter/safe-realization proof, E-18 interfaces and E-40 words remain.


## EV-129 — Proposed SHA3-256 bytes-only profile and byte-event tariff

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED SHA3-256/32-byte profile and byte-event tariff; Codex C's bounded proposal/rebase review is SOUND; exact R17 independent review and adoption remain pending.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an obtained argument under HashProfile sha3-256-fips202-2015-bytes-v1, defined with the complete byte-interface block attached to this rule

PROPOSED — Define profile identifier `sha3-256-fips202-2015-bytes-v1`, referring to SHA3-256 in FIPS202, August2015, sections on KECCAK and SHA3-256, with the following byte interface. FIPS202 is the normative reference; an eventual revised standard does not silently update this identifier. Its publication page was read as HTML; the full standard PDF was not opened under this card's restriction. [NIST FIPS202](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/fips/202/final), accessed2026-11-12.

PROPOSED — Let b be any finite bytes value, with length n any mathematical natural, including0. Retain byte order. Byte b[i] supplies bits m[8i+j]=floor(b[i]/2^j) mod2 for j=0,…,7. Apply SHA3-256: KECCAK[512](m || 01,256), with1600 state bits,1088 rate bits and24-round Keccak-f[1600]. The initial state is zero. Append the domain bits01, then pad10*1: a1, the least nonnegative number of zero bits making the padded length a multiple of1088, and a final1. Absorb each padded block by XOR into the rate portion and apply the permutation. Return the first256 squeezed bits. Equivalently for bytes: append delimiter06, zero-fill the last136-byte block and XOR80 into its final byte; at n mod136=135 that final byte is86. At an exact rate boundary allocate a new padding block. [Keccak designers' specification summary](https://keccak.team/keccak_specs_summary.html), accessed2026-11-12; summary is explanatory, not normative.

PROPOSED — Consecutive eight-byte lane payloads are little-endian; lane index is x+5y. Output byte i packs squeezed bits8i,…,8i+7 with bit8i least significant. Preserve the resulting32 bytes in their published order. Hex in this package displays each byte conventionally; it is neither an input encoding nor an integer interpretation. [Keccak designers' byte conventions](https://keccak.team/keccak_bits_and_bytes.html), accessed2026-11-12.

INFERENCE — The empty message absorbs one padding block:06,134 zero bytes,80. For n bytes the padded block count is b(n)=floor(n/136)+1; a136-byte input therefore takes two permutations before the32-byte output. A200-byte input contains more than one rate block. These are consequences of the stated padding, not benchmark results.

PROPOSED — `hash(b)` returns that mathematical bytes value of length32, retaining every leading zero byte. No key, personalization, application prefix, text conversion, length encoding or other framing is inserted by the callable primitive. Application identity encodings supply their own explicit framing before calling this same function. Keccak-256 and SHAKE256 are different functions and are excluded from this profile.

PROPOSED — The input domain contains all finite bytes values, with no greatest length. Streaming or finite-resource implementations must not wrap a host counter or introduce a mathematical length cap; inability to represent, admit, budget or allocate a particular call follows the separately specified resource/stop contract. It is not ordinary wrong-kind none. No implementation can promise successful evaluation of every finite input on finite hardware.

- from: SOURCE A2_R1:28,40-42,49-52,87-108,118-119,144-147,156-157,175-179,189; G0182:17,27,35,53-55,63; G_A2_R0:132,162; DESIGN_R5:912,919; PLAN:99,106-107 SOURCE — PROPOSAL-HASH.md:17–27,175–249; CODEX-C-HASH-PROPOSAL-REVIEW REPORT.md:1–35, SHA-256 9c81299c63efb9a377f8149b2c69bed2126fbc5fde6d53bf9cfec4ee7a5131a4, verdict SOUND for proposal/rebase, no adoption; owner-record-2026-11-12-hash-sha3-bytes.json and REGISTER:38,50.
- text: PROPOSED For any finite bytes b of length n, hash(b) is FIPS202 August2015 SHA3-256 under the attached HashProfile definition, as a bytes value of length32. There is no mathematical input-length cap. Use (A,R,T,W)=(n+1,33,0,n+1); W is n input-byte events plus one finish event, and the own invoice is2n+35. T0 is a semantic-frame entry, not a zero-scratch-space claim. Classify nonbytes first: actual-parts Rule E applies and paid success returns existing bare none, without canonical encoding or hashing of that argument. Compute/compare the complete uncapped invoice safely and pay it atomically after argument evaluation, before primitive work/construction. Full prospective R is paid even on canonical reuse. Earlier completed argument effects/debits remain. Evidence-package SHA-256 remains separate.
- case: PROPOSED T18 gives the copied NIST empty32-byte answer with own invoice35; T20 names SHA3-256(B[00]) with own invoice37; T19's former natural carrier is retired. T21 returns existing none with own invoice2 for an obtained admitted row. Exact/one-less own-fuel controls follow the N11 qualification; no whole-run budget or implementation pass is claimed.
- excludes: PROPOSED Keccak256, SHAKE256, natural-carrier conversion, reordered output bytes, implicit text encoding or application framing, nonbytes overload, unreviewed host length cap, partial debit, reuse discount, inferred scope-byte authority, second payment for prepaid W or claimed native-work/calibration proof.
- gaps: PROPOSED Independent profile review/adoption, vector-origin qualification and implementation qualification remain. E-3/E-27 retain complete safe-calculation/covered-work and physical realization obligations. E-12 retains admission/host-resource/outcome integration. E-16/E-23 retain complete canonical identity/start/profile/domain/import/replay bytes; E-38's later nonbytes overload remains separate. The block-aware tariff is an alternative proposal, not this entry's schedule.

PROPOSED — Byte-framed scope representation does not identify its hash profile. A containing immutable profile descriptor must give the context of every such name, including names nested in sets/rows/cases. Different-profile names require a separate reviewed admission/equality contract before mixing; representable bytes alone authorize no import.

## EV-135 — Own arithmetic in programs and independent development checking

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — SOURCE the shipment/check policy is owner-decided; interface, checker and evidence details remain open. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bignum-check-lookback-working.json; question 2]
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build and every program using Loom big-number arithmetic; the development check of that arithmetic
- from: SOURCE OWN_BIGNUM_LOOKBACK Q2; ONE_BRIEF:66,71–72,111,460–464; R13:837,1297 (under review)
- text: SOURCE Loom writes its own big-number code for every program; no other big-number implementation ships. During development that code is checked against an independent implementation behind one narrow arithmetic interface. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bignum-check-lookback-working.json; question 2] INFERENCE Two builds sharing one arithmetic implementation can share one error; their agreement alone is not an independent arithmetic check. The independent implementation is a development checker, not a runtime fallback or shipping dependency. Existing exact arithmetic, resource/precharge, edge and clean-stop obligations remain. PROPOSED Concrete interface, independent implementation/version, inputs, observations and comparison evidence still need specification and review; no particular library or Python checker is selected.
- case: PROPOSED NOT RUN. Two evaluator builds with the same wrong arithmetic answer agree with one another; that agreement does not discharge the independent development check. A separate implementation behind the narrow interface supplies the required comparison, whose matched inputs/results and limits must be recorded. Shipping only Loom arithmetic remains a distinct obligation from obtaining a successful comparison. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bignum-check-lookback-working.json; question 2]
- excludes: SOURCE / INFERENCE Shipping the independent checker, treating shared code as independent, adopting a library/version or runtime fallback from this decision, altering existing arithmetic equations/tariffs, or claiming correctness from a finite check alone. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-bignum-check-lookback-working.json; question 2]
- gaps: E-15 retains general build independence, full observations/correspondence and records, plus the exact arithmetic interface/checker/corpus and evidence. No runtime, qualification or phase completion is asserted.

## EV-136 — Conditional internal set-search comparison events and frames

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT - PROPOSED transcription and explication of the pinned comparison schedule; no tariff adoption, whole-meter completion or physical-storage claim.
- kind: requirement
- subject: each comparison of a nonempty member, insert or remove search on admitted finite acyclic immutable values under all EV-039 identity, canonical-set, equal-value interning and atomic-sequence-handle premises
- from: SOURCE sequences and values R3 REPORT.md, SHA-256 7071b259ddba8f6eda4c189e1f4ce2f6f0a7351e12955209ab82e0004eac0f28, lines 70,77-98,107; sequences and values R4 REPORT.md, SHA-256 b91ff85fa830319381f1f908128c1fff15f32d56fcca6e6a7cf69b364072b33d, lines 84-88; M6 ARCH-R6-S5.PROPOSED.md, SHA-256 29b2939d8e62be6343af85293d3b5b69960055c34f4abd14eb6b0e42ef4dac55, line 350; EV-039 and EV-088-091 of the bound R16-R1 evaluator rules. R14 D-VAL-7 is prospective and is not used as an existing source.
- text: PROPOSED Under the subject's premises, use EV-039's internal order and the halving search with lowest, highest and midpoint rounded down. Begin a comparison by visiting its operand pair, at charge 1. If it is the same node, it is equal with no further charge or descent. Otherwise different kinds decide in EV-039's order with no further charge. For two values of the same kind, add the following own-key work: naturals, the shorter bit length, with zero having bit length 1; byte values, the shorter byte length; cases, the shorter name length plus the shorter bit length of the two counts plus the shorter bit length of the two case ordinals; sequences, the shorter scope length plus the shorter bit length of the two creation counts; rows and sets, zero. Compare own keys in EV-039's order: natural magnitude; byte length then bytes; row length; complete choice identity then ordinal; set cardinality; stable sequence identity. A differing own key ends comparison. Otherwise visit corresponding payload, fields or canonical ordered members as pairs, left to right, stopping at the first difference. Each active pair counts one logical frame from its visit until settled. Retain that frame throughout every visited child, including the last; release a child before its next sibling. Comparing own metadata adds no frame, and the search's lowest/highest/midpoint adds none. P never exceeds the smaller operand height. Continue to charge EV-088's current once-per-search T formula, not observed P. Search W sums the comparisons' events. EV-090's atomic known invoice and EV-091's one-unit paid-event prefix apply: a multi-unit own-key work term is not an all-or-nothing dynamic precheck. Failure retains earlier paid events and commits no unfinished operation result or state change.
- case: PROPOSED Component fixtures with obtained operands and a singleton search: Row(Row(0)) versus Row(Row(0),0) has heights 3,3, W=1, P=1, T=4; Row(Row(0)) versus Row(Row(1)) has heights 3,3, W=4, P=3, T=4; Row() versus Row(0) has heights 1,2, W=1, P=1, T=2. These are component observations, not complete-program fuel or admission fixtures.
- excludes: A further frame or event for row lengths; field descent before differing lengths decide; release of a parent for its last child; using actual P in place of EV-088's T; inspecting sequence contents; extending public compare to mixed kinds; refunding an already paid comparison prefix.
- gaps: This conditional schedule does not complete K-1/K-2 identities, canonical bytes/profile/hash/collision or admission validation, E-40 spellings, other primitive or positive-loop invoices, separate admission resources, whole-program integration, EV-018/021 unavailable-bound evidence, incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration evidence or physical calibration. Existing gaps remain except the precisely replaced missing-schedule qualifications. R14 exact preimages and postimage are not authenticated by this entry. Removing the reservation's plus 1 is a separate tariff proposal requiring its own review and applicable authority.

SOURCE — Exact entry body above: CODEX-X-ARCH-FRAMES-REVIEW REPORT.md:64–74, SHA-256 3dde4ee77ca4df351dcc37f475bb1372fe026458d304fae806d3bc1a772546b9. Original SV3/S4/M6 sources are attributed through that reviewed package and were not opened for R17.

PROPOSED. Keep the current search reservation formula in the frame clarification. Under the bound schedule its final plus 1 is unused margin, not a counted comparison or search-driver frame. Removing only that final 1 is a separate numbered tariff proposal: each nonempty member, insert or remove invocation would pay 1 less once, including duplicate insert and absent remove. The redraw set includes EV-088's formula/example; EV-100/102's inherited terms and all affected explicit and composed fuel receipts; the R14/R17 bound, case and exclusion wording; M6's paper examples, repeated summaries, slack ranges and prefix/cutoff behavior; and new versioned source/program evidence for the bound. For the 1,000-member duplicate insert, preinvoice/total 1014/1095 become 1013/1094 and the no-R control 93 becomes 92. Scope-name totals 38/39 become 37/38; at fixed fuel 38 both would finish, and the one-short second-case fixture must instead use fuel 37. RF-3's T42/slack41 become T41/slack40. The looseness range starts at 0. Empty searches and independently established non-search T=0 operations are unaffected. Codex S's files were not supplied to this review, so their asserted status is NOT VERIFIED. Prepare and independently review the full changed bytes and applicable authority before adoption; this review authorizes no tariff application or owner decision.
